# WooWop lane — GoW bootable + playable drive

Ben's directive, 2026-11-20 ~01:35 EST: work until GoW boots and plays on the
Series S. WooWop (Ben's Muse) is now driving alongside Codex Cloud.
## Who owns what
- Codex owns: loader/build cycle in tiramisu-cycle-recovery (source edits,
  out-recovered-gow, 1.x builds), and ALL Xbox package deploys (it holds the
  Device Portal credentials).
- WooWop owns: (1) game-file transfer completion to the Xbox, (2) 0.2.2.83
  source reconciliation, (3) verification + proof. WooWop reaches the Xbox
  through the Tiramisu app server (10.0.0.23:9127, no portal creds needed)
  for logs/games/launch/uploads.
## WooWop will NOT
- Edit tiramisu-cycle-recovery source while a Codex build is running.
- Deploy/install/uninstall packages on the Xbox.
- Delete game data or unrelated apps on the Xbox, or projects on mineplex.
- Touch out-recovered-gow or the 1.x build .rc/.log files.
- Bypass DRM or copy game contents out of the authorized paths.
## Shared record
- gow-real-exe-audit.md stays the source of truth for state.
- WooWop's loop runs every 30 min; it checks for Codex activity (recent
  progress files, running builds) before touching shared state, and logs
  its own steps here.
- Handoff needed from Codex: deploy the fixed transfer build
  (out-gow-transfer, rc 0 at 22:00 Nov 19 — dir currently missing, rebuild
  if needed) so large uploads report errors honestly; and Xbox free-space
  readings when convenient.

## 2026-11-20 ~03:10 EST — crash audit (WooWop, read-only, bridge_steam.cpp + bridge_kernel32.cpp + cycle log)
- RULED OUT: ISteamUser has NO string-returning methods at all (slots 0-7: int/bool/u64/void/bool+buffer; rest noop). The NULL string CANNOT come from the Steam User vtable. Flat Steam getters return real objects (not NULL). GetCommandLineW/A return a real quoted exe path. GoW imports only 9 steam_api64 fns (no GetSteamInstallPath).
- RULED OUT (startup crash): GoW opens NO files before the fault — 0 CreateFile lines in the whole cycle log, and file opens ARE logged (NtCreateFile). Missing-file hypothesis is WEAK for the startup crash (transfer still needed for playability after boot).
- LIVE CANDIDATE: GetStartupInfoW returns a ZEROED STARTUPINFOW (fill_startup_info_w memsets 104 bytes, sets only cb=104) — lpDesktop=NULL, lpTitle=NULL. Real Windows fills lpDesktop="WinSta0\Default" and lpTitle=exe name. If GoW reads lpTitle/lpDesktop and passes it to a string fn -> exactly this fault (NULL read, CRT string fn, SWAR magic in R10). RACCOIN tolerates the zeroed struct (Unity doesn't read those fields); GoW may.
- TLS healthy in 0.0.1.13 trace (callbacks=1, block 4592, index 1025, array+block allocated). Fault chain: entry 0x3ec680 -> +0x3ec520 -> ucrtbase+0x707d6 -> +0x3ffaa7 -> +0x40a983 -> +0x40bf6a -> fault +0x4b113b. NOTHING logged between GetSteamID and the fault.

NEVER called pre-fault: GetCurrentDirectory, GetEnvironmentVariableA/W, GetTempPath, GetWindowsDirectory, GetSystemDirectory, RegQueryValueEx, GetUserName, GetComputerName, all Steam string methods.
- Steam: ONLY SteamUser021 requested; only vtable call is GetSteamID -> 0110000100000001. Vtable fix (GetSteamUILanguage->"english", UserStats/Friends->"", Input wired) CONFIRMED orthogonal — GoW never calls those pre-fault.

- CRT ID: ucrtbase.dll+0x707d6 = _initterm (initializer-table walker), medium-high confidence; fault is inside a GoW static initializer (0x3ffaa7) during the _initterm walk, before WinMain.
- NEXT CANDIDATE: guest env block has ONLY 8 vars (PATH, TEMP, TMP, USERPROFILE, APPDATA, LOCALAPPDATA, COMPUTERNAME, USERNAME — bridge_kernel32.cpp:2037-2042). GoW uses the REAL ucrtbase.dll, whose _wgetenv reads the PEB env block directly — bypassing bridged GetEnvironmentVariableW (0 calls, consistent). A missing standard var (SystemRoot, ProgramData, PUBLIC, ProgramFiles, HOMEDRIVE/HOMEPATH, OS, PATHEXT, NUMBER_OF_PROCESSORS, PROCESSOR_ARCHITECTURE, ALLUSERSPROFILE) read by the initializer -> NULL -> strlen(NULL) at 0x4b113b. Next build (0.2.2.95): expand env block to a full standard Windows set; if GoW boots, that was it.

Gate: tools/build-test.sh rc=0 (1273 PASS lines, 50x "RESULT: ALL PASS (0 failures)", 0 failures). rcs: woowop-build-8.rc / woowop-gate-8.rc (both 0). Two UWP-only compile fixes along the way (desktop-partition APIs undeclared under WINAPI_FAMILY_APP): timeBeginPeriod/timeEndPeriod + TerminateThread are now declared directly in bridge_winmm.cpp / main.cpp / bridge_thread.cpp; winmm.lib pulled in via #pragma comment(lib) — links clean.
- What landed: (1) g_main_image tracked by trt_images_register_main; trt_images_first() returns the exe, not slot 0 (fixes GetModuleHandle(NULL) + FindResource/EnumResource/LoadString(NULL)); (2) stream_to_file checks every fwrite + fclose, stats the file and verifies on-disk size == Content-Length before ok:true (mismatch → remove + 500), success JSON now {"ok":true,"size":N}; (3) load-depth cap 8 → 32; (4) TerminateThread really terminates (::TerminateThread on Windows, pthread_cancel on Linux) + sets the exit code; (5) TLS AddressOfCallbacks==0 guard (zero callbacks, not a load failure) + log, cap 64 → 256 with a truncation log; (6) null-check img in trt_images_by_handle / trt_images_name (pending-slot race); (7) ExpandEnvironmentStringsW expands %VAR% tokens (unknown/unterminated copied verbatim); (8) GetStartupInfoA builds real ANSI strings (was: reused the W fill, UTF-16 pointers in an ANSI struct); (9) GetModuleFileNameW honors the handle (DLL → exe dir + DLL name; synthetic → System32 + name; NULL/exe → exe path); (10) MultiByteToWideChar does proper CP1252 / UTF-8 (incl. surrogate pairs) instead of 2 UTF-16 units per byte >= 0x80 (also fixes a latent overrun for callers sizing from the size query, e.g. bridge_ntdll); (11) registry hive seeded: HKLM\SOFTWARE\Valve\Steam (InstallPath/SteamPath/Language), ...\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion (ProductName/CurrentVersion/CurrentBuild/DisplayVersion/ProgramFilesDir/CommonFilesDir, matching the 10.0.19045 personality), ...\Microsoft\DirectX (Version 4.09.00.0904 + InstalledVersion) — persisted hive values still win over seeds; (12) host timeBeginPeriod(1) at boot (boot_all) + guest timeBeginPeriod/timeEndPeriod forwarded to the real API on Windows; (13) HeapReAlloc charges only max(0, want-old), discharges on shrink after success; (14) budget_bytes_once is a real once (function-local static); (15) soft 80/90% flags re-arm on discharge below threshold + over-discharge clamp logs as a BUDGET ACCOUNTING tripwire; (16) worker-thread faults logged like the main-thread filter (code/address/regs/stack, "guest fault [worker ...]" prefix); (17) /api/status gains guest_running (running_game non-empty); running unchanged; (18) case-insensitive game-name matching (_stricmp at all 3 shelf comparisons + xfer_ieq in webxfer_find_game); (19) _fseeki64/_ftelli64 in log-tail readers (webxfer + host_log; plain fseek/ftell on LP64 Linux where long is 64-bit). Also fixed the stale host_log.h comment (web /api/logs reads the file, not the ring).
- Files changed (14): src/trt/images.cpp, images.h, trt.h, pe_loader.cpp, bridge_thread.cpp, bridge_sys.cpp, bridge_kernel32.cpp, bridge_membudget.cpp, bridge_winmm.cpp, bridge_reg.cpp, src/host/webxfer.cpp, main.cpp, host_log.cpp, host_log.h. - Spike validated Path B's two unknowns OFFLINE on mineplex (read-only; no Tiramisu tree touched, no Tiramisu builds, no console touch, no commit/push). All work in /tmp scratch, cleaned up afterwards. No apt installs (used system Mono 6.8.0.105 + dotnet SDK 8.0.130 + mcs already on the box).

Game boots: FNA3D/OpenGL + MojoShader glsl120, GFX/MTN load, GAME DISPLAYED, full DONE LOADING, title screen renders (~142% CPU, ~100s stable under Xvfb llvmpipe). Instrumented run proves 13/14 .xnb load via FNA (12 Effects + MonocleDefault SpriteFont; only Effects/Border not yet loaded, loads on level entry). Content surface is tiny: only 14 .xnb (all XNB v5, platform 'w', uncompressed, EffectReader/SpriteFontReader); other 1197 files are raw (.data atlases, .png, .bank, .bin) loaded by game code, not the content pipeline.

Steam Init/IsSteamRunning must return true or the game aborts with 'Steam not found!'. FMOD out-params must return plausible handles/lengths (GetPath retrieved=0 crashes GetEventName with count=-1).
- Unknown 2 RESOLVED: Game.dll references 84 WinForms/Drawing members (form creation, Application.Run + Idle pump, WndProc, Screen, Cursor, MessageBox) but ALL WinForms use is INTERNAL to Game.dll (WindowsGameForm/Host/Window are internal types) and FNA has ZERO WinForms references (one doc-comment URL only). Full FNA substitution means ZERO WinForms work. Keeping real Game.dll instead would need a medium-large CoreWindow-backed WinForms driver (form + pump + WndProc + Screen). Not recommended.
- Licenses (all permissive, no GPL issue unlike Boxedwine GPL-2 for Path A): FNA MS-PL, FNA3D zlib, FAudio zlib, MojoShader zlib, SDL2 zlib. FNA netstandard2.0 build does NOT run under mineplex Mono (no netstandard facade installed); the mcs/.NET 4.x build is the working path.
- RECOMMENDATION: Path B for Celeste. Remaining implementation: Mono embedding on UWP (Unity proves Mono-on-Xbox), FNA-as-MS-names + forwarder build (proven here), dllmap fmod/fmodstudio/CSteamworks to existing 64-bit bridges, custom Tiramisu_FNAPlatform backend (56-func table, ~25 needed for Celeste) against CoreWindow/XInput/VFS, FNA3D D3D11 driver adapted to the host device/CoreWindow swapchain (no HWND on UWP). Path A (emulate 32-bit CLR under x86 engine) remains the general solution for 32-bit .NET games but is very large/open-ended and unnecessary for Celeste.
- First implementation step: embed Mono in the 64-bit host + load the FNA-as-MS-names stack offline (harness test: Celeste.exe reaches Game.RunGame() with stub bridges), then port the FNAPlatform backend.

Two real bugs found by the harness (both fixed, both documented in code):
- Steam stub `RestartAppIfNecessary` returned true (generated bool default) -> Celeste.Main exits silently ("Steam is relaunching you"). Now false, with WasRestartRequested + RemoteStorage FileExists/FilePersisted also false.
- Entering Main via mono_runtime_invoke leaves Assembly.GetEntryAssembly() NULL -> NRE in Monocle.Engine..cctor. mono_jit_exec sets the entry assembly (the CLI's path). Base dir also had to be set (content is BaseDirectory-relative).

## 2026-11-20 ~11:05 EST — FNA3D D3D11 host-device adaptation (Path B step 3) [fna3d1]
**Tree:** `~/tiramisu-woowop-engine-fna3d` (base: mono tree, master 8019487 + Mono embedding).
**What:** FNA3D's D3D11 driver now accepts Tiramisu's host-provided `ID3D11Device` + CoreWindow swapchain instead of creating its own.
- New entry points: `FNA3D_CreateDeviceWithDevice(params, debugMode, device, context, swapchain)` (explicit init path) and `FNA3D_D3D11_SetHostDevice(device, context, swapchain)` / `FNA3D_D3D11_ClearHostDevice()` (pre-registration: stock FNA managed code calling plain `FNA3D_CreateDevice` adopts the host device with ZERO managed-code/platform-layer changes; one-shot, consumed by the first CreateDevice).
- Host swapchain is registered as the default render target; `FNA3D_SwapBuffers` Presents on it. FNA3D never creates/resizes/recreates/destroys the host swapchain; RTV format follows the host desc (BGRA8).
- New host handoff API `src/trt/fna3d_host.{h,cpp}`: `trt_fna3d_attach{,_from_hooks}` / `trt_fna3d_detach` / `trt_fna3d_is_attached` / `trt_fna3d_last_error`. Ownership: the host ALWAYS owns device/context/swapchain; FNA3D AddRefs at adopt and Releases at destroy; host must keep them alive attach→detach and must NOT Present or resize the swapchain while a game runs (the game's `FNA3D_SwapBuffers` IS the present).
- UWP build: `tools/build-host.sh` compiles `FNA3D.c`, `FNA3D_Driver_D3D11.c`, `FNA3D_PipelineCache.c`, a minimal SDL2-compat shim (`third_party/fna3d-src/sdl-shim/`, real Win32 impls, honestly-absent window APIs) and `fna3d_host.cpp` into the host. MojoShader is NOT compiled separately — it already lives in the host via `src/trt/mojo_hlsl.c`, extended with the effects + d3d11 backend (WinRT branch: direct `D3DCompile`, no LoadLibrary). Explicit obj check (section 2b) fails the build if the driver objects are missing.
- Linux harness unchanged: OpenGL driver via llvmpipe; gate still passes `PASS mono/celeste`.
**Verification (offline):** `tools/build-host.sh out-woowop-engine-fna3d1` rc=0 (UWP exe 3,487,232 B; DllCharacteristics 0x9160 AppContainer; FNA3D D3D11 driver objects present; exe imports d3d11.dll + D3DCOMPILER_47.dll); `tools/build-test.sh` rc=0, 1288 PASS lines, 5x "RESULT: ALL PASS (0 failures)" incl. `PASS mono/celeste: GAME DISPLAYED + DONE LOADING` (Celeste title screen under embedded Mono, libFNA3D.so.0 rebuilt from the modified sources). rc files: `~/tiramisu-gow-skye/docs/progress/woowop-build-fna3d1.rc`, `woowop-gate-fna3d1.rc` (both 0). Notes: first gate run failed only on a stale CMakeCache copied from the mono tree (fixed by removing the two stale build dirs, not a code issue); first UWP build failed only on a `out_of_memory` name collision in the MojoShader unity build (fixed by renaming the d3d11 backend's file-local static to `d3d11_out_of_memory`, 3 lines in `third_party/mojoshader/mojoshader_d3d11.c`; fna3d-src copy keeps the upstream name).
**Console-verification-pending (NOT verified):** runtime D3D11 rendering on the Xbox; the host-swapchain adopt path; Present driving the frame loop; flip-model GetBuffer(0) interplay between the host UI RTV and the game's RTV; effect/shader compilation via D3DCompile on the console.
**TODO for the FNA platform-layer agent:** no platform-layer change is REQUIRED — pre-register the host device via `trt_fna3d_attach_from_hooks()` before the managed game creates its GraphicsDevice and stock FNA works. Alternatively the platform layer may call `FNA3D_CreateDeviceWithDevice` directly with the host handles. Also: `FNA3D_Image.c` is NOT in the UWP build yet (needs SDL surfaces — follow-up if FNA image loading needs it); the host UI loop must not Present/resize the swapchain during a game run.

## 2026-11-20 23:02 EST — ROOT CAUSE OF SILENT ABORT FOUND (opencode)
- .126 SIGABRT handler + stderr redirect produced the FIRST real error: mono-stderr.log (328 B) says:
  `* Assertion: should not be reached at .../mono-6.8.0.105/mono/utils/mono-dl-windows.c:81`
- Line 78-82 = the `#else g_assert_not_reached()` branch of `mono_dl_open_file(NULL)` (the __Internal path: `HAVE_API_SUPPORT_WIN32_GET_MODULE_HANDLE` is undefined/0 in this UWP build). Also the named-DLL branch at lines 59-64 has the same problem (`HAVE_API_SUPPORT_WIN32_LOAD_LIBRARY`=0 AND `HAVE_API_SUPPORT_WIN32_LOAD_PACKAGED_LIBRARY`=0 -> assert). Those macros are defined in mono/metadata/w32subset.h but mono-dl-windows.c does NOT include it, and the build's config.h/common_defs never define them.
- So EVERY native library resolution attempt (our dllmaps -> __Internal, and any plain DllImport) ABORTS the process via g_assert_not_reached. That is the deterministic death right after Steamworks.NET loads and before the first pinvoke. NOT a code-integrity kill, NOT a trampoline problem.
- FIX: add `/DHAVE_API_SUPPORT_WIN32_GET_MODULE_HANDLE=1 /DHAVE_API_SUPPORT_WIN32_LOAD_PACKAGED_LIBRARY=1` to common_defs in third_party/mono-uwp/build-mono-uwp.sh, rebuild libmonosgen-uwp.lib (~3 min), rebuild host, pack, deploy. ### .126 TEST — ROOT CAUSE CAUGHT (04:00 UTC)
Celeste launch 202 queued; :9127 dark within 5 s. Trace (/tmp/t126.log,
5,291 lines) + mono-stderr.log (328 B — NOT empty this time):
- `126 diag: stderr->mono-stderr.log ok, stdout ok`

  VEH 0, DllImport 0, steam-door 0)

  the death is not finalization-driven. **Candidate 2 CONFIRMED** with the
  exact site: mono-dl-windows.c:81.

### Root cause: mono_dl_open_file NULL branch compiled out
mono-dl-windows.c:81 is the `g_assert_not_reached()` in the `#else` of
`#if HAVE_API_SUPPORT_WIN32_GET_MODULE_HANDLE` in `mono_dl_open_file`'s
file==NULL branch. `HAVE_API_SUPPORT_WIN32_GET_MODULE_HANDLE` was
classic-only in w32subset.h (line 175), so on UWP it was 0 and the
`__Internal` lookup path (pinvoke_probe_for_module → mono_dl_open(NULL))
hit the assert. The FIRST pinvoke (Steamworks `Init`, CSteamworks →
__Internal dllmap) died there — before any native call, before any
DllImport trace. Object-file proof: the lib's mono-dl-windows.obj has
`__imp_LoadPackagedLibrary` + `monoeg_assertion_message` but NO
`__imp_GetModuleHandleW`.
- MYSTERY (resolved): build-mono-uwp.sh DOES pass
  `/DHAVE_API_SUPPORT_WIN32_GET_MODULE_HANDLE=1` (common_defs) — but the
  lines are an UNCOMMITTED working-tree change vs 20a4662, and the 21:52
  EST lib object predates their effect (stamp confusion — exact sequence
  unknown). A wrapper reproduction with the current script DOES produce
  `__imp_GetModuleHandleW`, so the -D works when the lib is actually
  rebuilt from the current script. Do not trust "up to date" blindly.
- Patch 16 precedent: GET_STD_HANDLE / SIGNAL_OBJECT_AND_WAIT got the exact
  same treatment (classic-only → classic||UWP) for the exact same reason.

### Patch 33 (Mono fix — the actual fix for the silent death)
`33-uwp-getmodulehandle.patch` (in third_party/mono-uwp/patches/):
w32subset.h line 175 `#if G_HAVE_API_SUPPORT(HAVE_CLASSIC_WINAPI_SUPPORT)`
→ `... || G_HAVE_API_SUPPORT(HAVE_UWP_WINAPI_SUPPORT)`. Dry-run applies
cleanly to the staged tree (offset -2). README entry added. This makes the
fix durable in the patch set regardless of the command-line -D.
NEXT: lib rebuild (MONO_UWP_REBUILD=1) → verify new obj has
__imp_GetModuleHandleW + patches 31/32 intact → build .127 → deploy → test.
Expected: the game proceeds past Steamworks.NET to the first pinvoke
(Init — now exported). Watch next for: GetFunctionPointerForDelegate JIT
(marshal.c:331) at Steamworks callback registration, and the FNA3D device
path (CreateDevice) after Init returns.

  `TypeInitializationException: The type initializer for 'Sys' threw an
  exception. ---> DllNotFoundException: System.Native`
  at `Interop+Sys.LChflagsCanSetHiddenFlag()` ← `Interop+Sys..cctor()`
  ← `FileSystem.FileExists` ← `File.Exists` ← `Monocle.ErrorLog.Write(e)`

  TypeInitializationException on the retry).

  .../mono/metadata/exception.c:1299` — Mono's unhandled-exception path
  ends in g_assert_not_reached() AFTER exit() (exit apparently returns on
  this build — secondary; fix the unhandled exception and this goes away).
  SIGABRT count 2 in the cumulative file (1 per run); VEH 0; steam-door 0
  (SteamAPI_Init not yet reached — Main dies in early init first).

### Root cause: System.Native has no provider on the console
- Packaged etc/mono/config: `<dllmap dll="System.Native"
  target="$mono_libdir/libmono-native.so" os="!windows" />` — the dllmap
  does NOT apply on Windows, so Mono looks for System.Native.dll → absent.
- On Linux it resolves to /usr/lib/libmono-native.so (present). The UWP
  lib does NOT include mono/native (mono-sources.txt has no mono/native
  sources; pal-icalls.c registers only Interop/Sys::Read + macOS RunLoop).
- Surface (monodis --implmap on the packaged framework): mscorlib 28 +
  System.dll 19 pinvokes, ~34 unique SystemNative_* entry points:
  ConvertErrorPlatformToPal, ConvertErrorPalToPlatform, StrErrorR,
  GetNonCryptographicallySecureRandomBytes, OpenDir, GetReadDirRBufferSize,
  ReadDirR, CloseDir, ReadLink, FStat2, Stat2, LStat2, Symlink, ChMod,
  CopyFile, GetEGid, GetEUid, LChflags, LChflagsCanSetHiddenFlag, Link,
  MkDir, Rename, RmDir, UTime, UTimes, Unlink (mscorlib) + INotifyInit,
  INotifyAddWatch, INotifyRemoveWatch, Close, FLock, Open, Poll,
  mono_pal_init (System.dll).
- Fix: host-side bridge, same pattern as the steam/fmod doors — dllmap
  "System.Native" → "__Internal" (host dllmaps: trt_mono_dllmap in
  mono_host.h:48, installed via the assembly-load hook) + new door file
  with REAL Win32/UWP bridges for the file-IO core (Stat/LStat/FStat,
  Open, Close, ReadDir, MkDir, Rename, Unlink, ...) + fail-visible stubs
  for the exotic ones (INotify, FLock, Symlink, LChflags, CopyFile).
  Layouts (FileStatus, DirectoryEntry, Error, OpenFlags) must match the
  managed definitions EXACTLY — authoritative source is monodis on
  /usr/lib/mono/4.5/mscorlib.dll + System.dll (the mcs .cs sources were
  not found in /dev/shm/monouwp; mono/native pal headers are NOT in the
  UWP cache). OPEN QUESTION for the implementer: FileStream reads —
  Sys.Read is an INTERNAL CALL registered by mono_pal_init (pal-icalls.c,
  NOT in the UWP lib); determine whether FileStream.Read goes through it
  and register/provide it if so (or confirm MonoIO covers reads).
- The bridge is UWP-only: gate the dllmap install so the Linux gate keeps
  using libmono-native.so (do NOT break the offline mono/celeste test).
- NEXT: specialist agent implements the bridge + builds .128 (manifest
  bump); celeste-continue-2 deploys + tests. After System.Native, watch
  for: steam-door Init call, GetFunctionPointerForDelegate JIT
  (marshal.c:331) at callback registration, FNA3D CreateDevice, then
  GAME DISPLAYED.

  Xan.Tiramisu_0.2.2.129_x64__x81jywntpvgne\error_log.txt" is denied.`
  at `System.IO.FileStream..ctor` → SIGABRT (exception.c:1299 assert).
  GAME DISPLAYED: 0. steam-door: 6 (cumulative).

### Diagnosis: error_log.txt goes to the read-only install dir
Monocle.ErrorLog writes `error_log.txt` via a RELATIVE path, resolved
against the process CWD — which is the UWP install dir
(S:\Program Files\WindowsApps\...), READ-ONLY in AppContainer. (The host
sets the AppDomain base dir to the game dir, but the process CWD is still
the install dir.) Whether ErrorLog ran because of an earlier exception or
at startup, the write itself is what killed the run.
FIX (host-side, one change): set the process CWD to the game dir
(LocalState\games\celeste) before launching the guest
(SetCurrentDirectory in the Mono launch path, before trt_mono_run — or in
main.cpp's launch flow). This fixes ALL relative-path access at once
(error_log.txt, saves, settings, content-relative reads), not just the
log. Narrower alternative (not recommended): redirect error_log.txt only.
NOTE: after the CWD fix, the NEXT failure (if any) will be visible in
error_log.txt itself in the game dir — the logger will finally be able to
write, so the ORIGINAL exception (if ErrorLog was logging one) becomes
readable from the game dir.
